### 138. Three tabs key an effect on the whole `tab()` input, which GEMINI.md forbids

P3 · Console and site · size XS · wbskt/Wbskt.Console#179

**Where:** `src/app/features/workspace/policies/policy-tab/policy-tab.ts:50` (`effect(() => this.applyRequest(this.tab()))`), `src/app/features/workspace/audit/audit-tab/audit-tab.ts:44` (`effect(() => this.bindTab(this.tab()))`), `src/app/features/workspace/workflows/workflow-tab/workflow-tab.ts:33` with `workflow-tab/services/workflow-page.service.ts:61` (`effect(() => this.sync(this.tab(), ...))`)

**Scenario:** `TabService` rebuilds every tab object on any mutation, so each of these effects re-runs whenever any tab is activated or any tab's data is written. None loops today (each write-back is guarded and settles after one pass), but GEMINI.md says "Never key an effect on the whole `tab()` input" and names `policy-tab` as an example of doing it right, which is no longer true. The workflow page's `sync` runs on every live comms row appended to an unrelated client tab.

**Fix:** Key each on `computed(() => this.tab().id)` (plus the one data field it needs) and wrap the body in `untracked()`.

**Confidence:** high that the effects read the whole input; the absence of a loop was checked by reading each write-back, not by running

### 143. `:latest` moves before the deploy and on manual runs from any branch, and the rehearsal and compose treat it as what production runs

P3 · Infra and CI · size S · #789

**Where:** .github/workflows/build-images.yml:625, :642 and :698 (`if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'`, so `workflow_dispatch` on a branch qualifies), :689-691 and :721-723 (tags include `:latest`), :613 (`PREVIOUS_IMAGE=.../wbskt-migrator:latest`), :551-557 (comment: the migrator production last ran); deploy/compose/docker-compose.yml (`${IMAGE_TAG:-latest}` on every host image)

**Scenario:** On 10 Nov the images were pushed with `:latest` and the deploy then failed, so `:latest` is now a release production never ran, and each pull request's upgrade rehearsal starts from that schema. The same happens when someone dispatches "Build images" on a feature branch to get images for a test: the next rehearsal upgrades from the branch's schema, and a VM whose `.env` has no `IMAGE_TAG` would pull the branch build on its next `up`.

**Fix:** Stop tagging `:latest` in `build-images.yml`; move the tag in `deploy.yml` after `deploy.sh` succeeds, and only for a run on master, so it means "what production runs".

**Confidence:** high (the conditions and tag lists were read twice; the 10 Nov case is in the run list)

### 144. The console builds and deploys a manual run from any branch to production

P3 · Infra and CI · size XS · wbskt/Wbskt.Console#182

The backend guards the same step with `github.event_name == 'push' && github.ref == 'refs/heads/master'` (wbskt/Wbskt `.github/workflows/build-images.yml:731`). Only an environment protection rule, which could not be read from here, would stop it.

**Fix:** Add `&& github.ref == 'refs/heads/master'` to both conditions.

**Confidence:** high for the workflow text; whether the `production-console` environment restricts branches is not known

### 145. No repository proposes dependency or action updates, and the scan gate does not say which package it refused

P3 · Infra and CI · size S · #789

**Where:** no `.github/dependabot.yml` or Renovate file in any of the six repositories; wbskt/Wbskt .github/workflows/build-images.yml:539-549

**Scenario:** A new advisory against a NuGet package first shows up as a red `scan` on whatever push happens next, on master as well as on pull requests, so an unrelated hotfix cannot deploy until the package is dealt with. The step prints only the paths of the affected projects, so the person who hits it has to restore locally to learn the package. The same absence means pinned actions and base images would never be bumped once #460 pins them.

**Fix:** Add `.github/dependabot.yml` with the `nuget`, `github-actions` and `docker` ecosystems (weekly, grouped) to wbskt/Wbskt and the matching ecosystems to the other repositories; print package, version, severity and advisory URL from `vuln.json`; run the scan nightly as well so an advisory appears before a deploy depends on it.

**Confidence:** high (file search in all six checkouts; whether Dependabot alerts are switched on in settings is not known)

### 146. The release jobs of the three libraries run third-party actions by a moving ref, and Arduino publishes with a long-lived token

P3 · Infra and CI · size XS · #789

**Where:** Wbskt.Python/.github/workflows/release.yml:53 (`pypa/gh-action-pypi-publish@release/v1` in the job that holds `id-token: write`); wbskt.dotnet/.github/workflows/release.yml:54 (`NuGet/login@v1`); Wbskt.Arduino/.github/workflows/release.yml:40 (`PLATFORMIO_AUTH_TOKEN`); Wbskt.Arduino/.github/workflows/ci.yml (`arduino/arduino-lint-action@v2`, `arduino/compile-sketches@v1`)

**Scenario:** A branch or tag ref on a third-party action can be moved or removed by whoever controls that repository; wbskt/Wbskt's master broke on exactly that on 11 Nov. In these three workflows the action runs inside the job that can publish the package, so a moved ref can publish a package under the project's name, and a published version cannot be replaced. The PlatformIO token does not expire and is account-wide.

**Fix:** Pin every action in the three `release.yml` files (and the `ci.yml` they reuse) to a commit SHA with the version in a comment, and widen #460 to the library repositories. Rotate the PlatformIO token on a schedule until PlatformIO offers trusted publishing.

**Confidence:** high

### 147. The website has no pull-request check and builds on an end-of-life Node

P3 · Infra and CI · size XS · wbskt/Wbskt.Console#182

**Where:** .github/workflows/deploy.yml (triggers are `push: master` and `workflow_dispatch` only), :28 (`node-version: 20`)

**Scenario:** A pull request that breaks the prerender is not built anywhere; the break is found on master, where the deploy fails and the site stays on the previous build. Node 20 left support in April 2026; the console builds on 22.

**Fix:** Add a `pull_request` trigger that runs `npm ci && npm run build` without the deploy job, and move to Node 22.

**Confidence:** high

### 148. The shared conformance check on protocol changes leaves out Arduino and the MQTT protocol document

P3 · Infra and CI · size S · #789

**Where:** .github/workflows/sdk-conformance.yml (matrix `sdk: [python, dotnet]`; the pull-request `paths` list has `Docs/Client.Protocol.md` but not `Docs/Client.Protocol.MQTT.md`)

**Scenario:** A change to the conformance suite or the protocol is checked against the Python and .NET clients before it merges, and against Arduino only when Arduino's own CI next runs, which pulls the suite from master. A suite change that Arduino fails turns Arduino's CI red after the fact. A change to the MQTT protocol document alone does not start the workflow.

**Fix:** Add `arduino` to the matrix (its harness builds with `make -C test/conformance`) and the MQTT document to `paths`.

**Confidence:** high

### 149. A failed backup leaves its full-size staged copy on the database volume, one more each night

P3 · Infra and CI · size XS · #789

**Where:** deploy/scripts/backup.sh:96-106 (`die` after `BACKUP DATABASE` when the verify or the copy out fails), :110 (the staged copy is removed only on the success path), :173 (start of run: `mkdir -p` only); deploy/scripts/restore.sh:107-117 and :171 (same for the staged restore file)

**Scenario:** The host disk under `BACKUP_DIR` is full, or the copy out of the container is interrupted. `BACKUP DATABASE` has already written an uncompressed `.bak` into `/var/opt/mssql/backups` on the `sql-data` volume; the script dies and leaves it there. The next night writes another under a new stamp. The data volume fills with copies that protect nothing, on the disk SQL Server needs, in exactly the situation (low disk) that caused the first failure. `backup-check.sh` does not look in the staging directory.

**Fix:** In `backup.sh`, remove `$STAGING_DIR/*.bak` at the start of a run and in a `trap ... EXIT`; do the same for the staged file in `restore.sh`; add a `backup-check.sh` line that fails when the staging directory is not empty.

**Confidence:** high (both scripts read in full; no cleanup on the failure paths)

### 150. Host images run as root

P3 · Infra and CI · size XS · #789

**Where:** deploy/docker/Dockerfile (46 lines, no `USER`)

**Scenario:** A flaw in a host that lets a caller run code runs it as root inside the container, with write access to the application files. The .NET runtime images ship an `app` user for this.

**Fix:** Add `USER $APP_UID` before `ENTRYPOINT`; the hosts listen on 8080 and 1883, above the privileged range.

**Confidence:** high

### 151. The project's copy of `dev.env` is missing the billing and gateway keys and still sets a key nothing reads

P3 · Infra and CI · size XS · wbskt/Wbskt.Console#182

**Where:** project files `deploy-env/dev.env` (names only); deploy/compose/.env.example:132-146 (the ten `DODO_*` keys) and its `GATEWAY_WS_REPLICAS`, `GATEWAY_MQTT_REPLICAS`, `MSSQL_PID`, `SQL_MEMORY_LIMIT_MB`; deploy/scripts/deploy.sh:276-283 (`require_complete_env`) and :363-419 (`report_drift`)

**Scenario:** The copy has none of the ten `DODO_*` keys, `GATEWAY_MQTT_REPLICAS`, `GATEWAY_WS_REPLICAS`, `MSSQL_PID` or `SQL_MEMORY_LIMIT_MB`, and still carries `SOCKET_REPLICAS`, which nothing has read since the socket service was renamed. If the VM's `.env` matches, gateway-ws runs at the default of one replica whatever `SOCKET_REPLICAS` says, and billing is unconfigured.

**Fix:** Refresh the copy from the VM, rename `SOCKET_REPLICAS` to `GATEWAY_WS_REPLICAS` on the VM, and have `report_drift` warn about keys in `.env` that compose does not read.

**Confidence:** medium (the copy was compared by name; the VM's file could not be read)

### Margin against the capacity files

Read: `/mnt/project-files/capacity/wbskt-capacity-estimate.html` and `/mnt/project-files/capacity/wbskt-hosting-plan.html` only. Cost assumption: the single VM of the hosting plan, about $27 a month on Hetzner CX43 (8 vCPU, 16 GB) or $62 to $89 on Azure B4as_v2, with the estimate's "optimised" column (about 4,000 msg/s on 8 vCPU, 2,000 on 4 vCPU; about €0.001 a device; about 35 MB of traffic rows per sustained msg/s), because the changes it assumes have since landed in the repo (the narrow `ClientTraffic` table at 2 days, the live feed by subscription, Express pinned with a memory cap); #446 and the load test #448 are still open, so none of it is measured. A plan's cost is its share of the VM at its full included usage: the dearest of its message share (Azure, pay as you go) and its per-device figure.

| Plan | Price / month (yearly as monthly) | Included messages as msg/s (at the 2x throttle) | Cost at full included usage | Gross margin | Traffic rows at full use (at 2x) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Free | $0 | 0.58 (throttled at 1x) | $0.004 to $0.03 | negative by design: about 1 to 3 cents an account | 0.02 GB |
| Maker | $5 ($4.17) | 3.9 (7.7) | $0.03 to $0.17 | about 96% | 0.13 GB (0.26) |
| Pro | $24 ($20) | 19.3 (38.6) | $0.13 to $0.86 | about 96% | 0.66 GB (1.3) |
| Team | $99 ($82.50) | 77.2 (154) | $0.52 to $3.43 | about 96% | 2.6 GB (5.3) |
| Business, 2,000 clients | $300 | 77.2 (never throttled, overage billed) | $0.52 to $3.43 | about 99% | 2.6 GB |

- No paid plan's included usage costs more than its price as a share of the VM. Free is the only plan whose included usage costs more than its price, which is what a free plan is; at €0.001 a device a full Free account is about 2 cents, and the 8 vCPU VM's memory ceiling (about 50,000 devices) is about 2,500 full Free accounts.
- With the estimate's unoptimised column (€0.005 to €0.025 a device, about 100 msg/s before the disk fills) the same plans cost up to $2.75, $13.75 and $55 a month, so the margin falls to about 45%, and one Team account at its allowance is 77% of that ceiling. This is why the optimisations had to land before Team is sold, and why #448 matters.
- The step cost is where the margin is thin (finding above): the hosting plan moves off the single VM at about 300 msg/s or a database near 8 GB, to a stack of $500 to $900 a month. Every self-serve plan brings about $1.25 to $1.30 a month per sustained msg/s at its allowance, so the accounts that fill 300 msg/s pay about $385 a month, and about $190 if they run at the uncharged 2x. Business brings $3.89 per msg/s ($1,166 at 300 msg/s) and clears it.
- Credits: a plan's credits spent wholly on email are about 180 (Free), 2,270 (Maker), 13,600 (Pro) and 54,500 (Team) emails a month. The hosting plan budgets $0 to $10 a month for the relay "at hobbyist volume", which one Pro account sending email at its allowance would pass on a free tier. This is an assumption outside the two files' numbers, noted, not a finding.

## Docs and tests that no longer match the code

### Auth and management

- Console `GEMINI.md` ("Live updates": payloads arrive "minus `ClientId` / `PolicyId` / `WorkspaceId`") and `src/app/core/signalr/signalr-events.model.ts:64-66` say the ids are stripped. They are not for interface-declared ids (first finding). Correct the code, not the docs.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Email/OutboundMailQueue.cs:11-13` says every message the host sends has a user-triggered way to ask for it again. The email-changed notice has none, and an invitation only from the inviter's side.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Email/OutboundMailDispatcher.cs:11-14` says the retry ladder is bounded so a broken relay cannot wedge the drain loop. That holds only for a relay that fails fast; a send has no timeout.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Management.Host/Program.cs:49-50` says a ClientAuth instance keeps the registrations "minus the console's background work". `EventLogRetentionService` still runs there.
- `Clients/Wbskt.Api.Client/README.md` says "Every endpoint has two methods". `WbsktManagementClient.cs` has none for `GET clients/counts`, `GET clients/{clientRef}/message-types`, `POST clients/{clientRef}/ota`, `DELETE clients/{clientRef}/commands/{commandId}` and `GET stats/messages`. In the same two files 58 management and 41 auth method summaries still name the unversioned path (`POST /api/client-registrations/initiate`) while the code beside them calls `/api/v1/...`.
- Console `GEMINI.md` describes seeding and ageing the status bar's message rate from `OnWorkspaceMessageRateEvent` and `GET .../stats/messages`. No console code reads either yet (probably console #148, the status bar).
- Missing tests, named in the findings: nothing serializes an event with the hub's JSON options (no test mentions `SignalRPrivate`, and the E2E suite only probes `/hubs/notifications/negotiate` for a 401); `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/ManagementHost/SocketHostStartedHandlerTests.cs` has three tests and none where a client's connect is newer than the started event; nothing tests two state reports applied out of order.

### Credits and usage

- `Docs/Billing.md:116-119`: the overrun bound ("what was left times the runs going at once") leaves out credits of runs that ended since the last post, and does not say how large the stated bound is (20 to 200 times the allowance).
- `Docs/Billing.md:125` and `Docs/Billing.md:146-157`: "held exactly" and "the workspace cannot pass its cap, however many runs it starts at once" do not hold for an ended run's unposted credits, a child's start cost, or a cap set while runs are going. `Docs/Billing.Policies.md:57-58` makes the same promise to customers.
- `Docs/Billing.md:295`, `Docs/API.Endpoints.md:607` and `Models/Wbskt.Management.Models/Usage.cs:23-32`: "a run is refused once `used` reaches `limit` plus `topUp`" is wrong once a pack has been drawn; `Usage.cs:27-28` still describes the month-end settlement removed by #680.
- `Docs/API.Endpoints.md:607`: `throttled` is described as true only for a Free account; `WorkspaceUsageService.cs:50` also sets it for Maker, Pro and Team at twice the allowance.
- `Databases/Wbskt.Database/Functions/TenantCreditGrantBalance.sql:1`: "not drawn by a settled month" names the settlement that no longer exists; grants are drawn as credits are posted.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Workflow.Engine.Host/HostedServices/CreditUsagePoster.cs:14-15`: "the lag here never lets a start through on credits already spent" is not true for runs that have ended.
- `Docs/Billing.md:71-73`: calls the removed (or written back) account copy of a deleted workspace harmless; it decides whether that workspace's month still counts.
- `Docs/Billing.md:133`: the caveat covers a contract changed by hand, not one that ends by its `endsAt`.
- `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/Runtime/DefaultCreditCostCalculatorTests.cs:15-103`: six tests named for result types (`Calculate_Returns1_ForForkResult` and so on) all assert that an unknown kind costs 1.0; each builds a `NodeExecutionResult` it never uses. They date from when the price took the result and say nothing about today's calculator.
- Missing tests: an ended run's unposted credits against a cap or the allowance; a retired workspace's month; a cap set with a run going; a crossing whose publish fails for good (`CreditUsagePosterTests.cs:88` covers only the retry that succeeds); a notice after a plan change in the same month.

### Billing and pricing

- Wbskt/Docs/Billing.md:49 says `TenantPlanService` is something "the payment webhook will call too". It does now, through `TenantBillingSync.ApplyAsync` (Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Billing/TenantBillingSync.cs:342). Wbskt/Docs/API.Endpoints.md:326 has the same stale "once billing lands, the payment webhook" for the internal plan route, which the webhook never calls.
- Wbskt/Docs/Billing.md:422-423 and Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Billing/IBillingNotifier.cs:9-10: "Nothing is claimed while no relay is configured or no one can be written to, so the notice goes out once someone can receive it." That holds only for notices that are raised again (`PaymentFailed` on each sync, `GraceEnding` hourly, the message notices). `CreditAllowance80/100`, `Downgraded` and `PackExpiring` are raised once; `BillingNotifier.NotifyAsync` returns without claiming and without throwing (BillingNotifier.cs:26-45), the consumer acknowledges the event, and the notice is never sent.
- Wbskt/Docs/Billing.md:34-36 says deriving a Business contract's limits from `contractClients` means "a contract cannot be priced on one count and sized on another". `BusinessContract.OverridesFor` (Models/Wbskt.Models/Billing/BusinessContract.cs:46-55) lets `limitOverrides` set `clients`, `messagesPerMonth` or `creditsPerMonth` beside `contractClients`, a Business plan can be set with no `contractClients` at all (TenantPlanService.cs:30-33 only validates it when present), and the contract's client count is not stored, only the overrides.
- Wbskt/Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/Tables/TenantSubscriptions.sql:8-9 describes `RefundedThrough` as "the period end at the time a subscription payment was refunded"; the code stores the payment's date plus one interval plus a day (SubscriptionPlan.cs:72-73).
- Wbskt/Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/Tables/BillingNotices.sql:3 names `dbo.BillingNotice_Claim`; the procedure is `dbo.BillingNotice_Record`.
- Wbskt/Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Billing/SubscriptionPlan.cs:33-34 says a subscription cancelled at the end of its period "is not [billing], so the tenant can pick another". Since be6c143 a second checkout is refused while that period is still paid for (TenantBillingService.cs:294-299).
- Wbskt/Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/AuthHost/BillingPaymentsTests.cs:492 is still named `A_tenant_already_subscribed_changes_its_plan_in_the_portal_not_a_second_checkout`; the plan is changed with `POST …/billing/plan` since c221352.
- Wbskt/Docs/Billing.md says nothing about currency or tax. The site says tax is added at checkout by the merchant of record (billing.html:11, terms.html:26); the docs should say prices are USD before tax and that Dodo's checkout may charge in a local currency, or say that it may not.
- Issue #661 (wbskt/Wbskt, "Change a subscribed tenant's plan in place instead of through the portal") is still open; c221352 shipped it. The roadmap's data block will be carrying it as open work.
- Tests missing: no test compares `PlanCatalog.BuiltIn` with the table in Docs/Billing.md, so the "one set of limits" rule is kept by hand in four files (PlanCatalog.cs, Billing.md, the site's plans.html and billing.html, plus members.html); no test for a timed-out (`TaskCanceledException`) refund, checkout or upgrade; no gateway test built from a recorded Dodo subscription payment (the refund tests hand-build `ProviderPayment` with a plan line); `TenantSubscriptions_GetToReview` is tested with one subscription per tenant only; there is no end-to-end test of checkout, webhook and plan change (the E2E billing folder holds `UsageE2ETests.cs` alone), which is understandable without a provider stand-in, but `DodoOptions.BaseUrl` exists for one.

### Tenants and access

- Docs/AccessControl.md:128-129 says a `UserPermissionsChangedEvent` makes "anything caching the user's effective set" drop it. Nothing consumes that event; the management cache is cleared by `WorkspaceAccessChanges` over Redis (Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Controllers/AnnouncesWorkspaceAccessChangeAttribute.cs:29-32), and accepting an invitation does not announce at all (Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Controllers/InvitationsController.cs), which is fine only because refusals are never cached.
- Docs/AccessControl.md:209-211 says `POST /api/v1/workspaces/{ref}/members` "also adds the user to the workspace's tenant". Lines 148-153 of the same file and `WorkspaceMember_Add.sql` (THROW 50009) say it no longer does.
- Docs/AccessControl.md:80-81 lists five default reads for the `User` role; Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/StoredProcedures/Tenant_Create.sql:58 grants seven (it adds `integrations.read` and `billing.read`).
- Docs/AccessControl.md:234-251 (management route table) names routes that no longer exist: `GET clients/policy/{ref}`, `PATCH clients/{ref}/status`, `PATCH clients/{ref}/name`, `POST clients/{ref}/command`. Today there is one `PATCH clients/{clientRef}` (Hosts/Wbskt.Management.Host/Controllers/ClientsController.cs:156), `?policyRefId=` and `POST clients/{clientRef}/commands` (line 309). The table also lacks integrations, readings, usage, credit cap, message types, the command list and cancel, drafts and test runs. Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/ManagementHost/ManagementEndpointPermissionTests.cs:21-92 is the true list.
- Docs/AccessControl.md:273-274 says nested-resource checks "answer 403 for both no such resource and not yours" and cites Coding.Conventions.md, which says 404 with the resource's `<RESOURCE>_NOT_FOUND`.
- Docs/Auth.Host.E2E.Scenarios.md:53-56 says registration is "one transaction" that creates the account and its tenant; Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/RegistrationService.cs:143-145 says the user insert and `Tenant_Create` are separate calls (the code side is open as #164, wbskt/Wbskt).
- Docs/Auth.Host.E2E.Scenarios.md:383 (`WS_MEM_06`) says adding an unregistered email "doubles as a registration oracle". It no longer does: an unknown address and a registered one outside the tenant both answer 404 `USER_NOT_FOUND` (Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/WorkspaceService.cs:93-124).
- Docs/Auth.Host.E2E.Scenarios.md:192 (`AUTH_RT_18`) and Tests/Wbskt.E2E.FeatureTests/Scenarios/Auth/AccountSecurityTests.cs:104-106 pin the replay false positive as expected behaviour (finding above).
- Tests/Wbskt.E2E.FeatureTests/Scenarios/Auth/WorkspaceLifecycleTests.cs:61-83 (`WS_CRD_04`) never asserts which tenant the member's workspace was created in (finding above).
- Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/ManagementHost/ManagementEndpointPermissionTests.cs:18 has the pre-`/api/v1` route prefix (finding above).
- Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Controllers/AuthController.cs:167-170: the `LogoutAll` summary says issued access tokens stay valid until they expire; Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/SignInService.cs:291-292 revokes them.
- Models/Wbskt.Auth.Models/LookupResponses.cs:123-131: the `CreatedInvitationResponse` summary says the codebase has no mail transport and the administrator sends the link; `TenantService.CreateInvitationAsync` queues the invitation mail (Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/AuthHost/InvitationMailTests.cs).
- Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/WorkspaceService.cs:54: the TODO says "today a user has exactly one tenant"; every invited user has two.
- Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/Functions/Tenant_Administrators.sql:4-5: says every management endpoint needs tenant-wide `users.manage`; the role, permission and assignment endpoints need `roles.manage` (Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Controllers/ManagementController.cs:115-158, 292-321).
- wbskt/Wbskt.Console GEMINI.md "Known backend gaps": says `WorkspaceResponse` is `(RefId, Name, Description, CreatedAt)`; it now also carries the tenant reference. The comment at src/app/features/dashboard/members-page/services/members.service.ts:183-186 repeats the old state.

### Gateways

Close codes, reason codes and message types, docs against code (check 1). "Doc" is `Docs/Client.Protocol.WebSocket.md` §4 or `Docs/Client.Protocol.MQTT.md` §1 and §4; constants are in `Clients/Wbskt.Protocol/WebSocketBinding.cs` and `MqttBinding.cs`, the mappings in `Hosts/Wbskt.Gateway.WebSocket.Host/Infrastructure/WebSocketCloseStatuses.cs` and `Hosts/Wbskt.Gateway.MQTT.Host/Infrastructure/MqttReasonCodes.cs`.

| Code | In the doc | Where the code sends it | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| WS 1000 Normal | yes | Only as the answer to a client's own Close | Matches; the server never starts a 1000 close |
| WS 1001 Restarting | yes, with the 0 to 30 s spread | Mapped from `ServerStopping` (`ClientSessions.cs:144`) but the socket is already aborted | **Documented, never sent** (finding above) |
| WS 1009 Too big | yes | `ClientInbox` size check | Matches; E2E `RAW_02` |
| WS 1011 Server error | yes | `Error` when the session throws | Matches by reading; no test against a real socket |
| WS 4001 Superseded | yes | `ConnectionManager` (same host), `ConnectionSupersededHandler` (other host) | Matches; E2E `MQTT_04` |
| WS 4002 Revoked | yes | `ClientAccessHandlers` | Matches. One code for revoked, deleted and secret rotated, which the doc says |
| WS 4003 Throttled | yes | `MessageBudget`, after 500 drops in a row | Matches; E2E `RAW_03`. The monthly-allowance throttle sends no code at all (finding above) |
| WS 4000 Updating (client) | yes | Read in `SocketHandler` | Matches |
| WS 1008 | "a gateway from before the 4001 to 4003 codes" (`WebSocket.md:68`) | Nowhere | Obsolete note: no such gateway is deployed; see Dead code |
| HTTP 401 / 403 / 400 on the upgrade | yes | `SocketHandler` (`CLIENT_TOKEN_INVALID`, `CLIENT_REVOKED`, `SOCKET_PROTOCOL_UNSUPPORTED`, `SOCKET_UPGRADE_REQUIRED`) | Matches; E2E `RAW_04` to `RAW_06` |
| MQTT CONNACK 0x86, 0x87, 0x85, 0x88 | yes | `MqttConnectAuthenticator` | Matches. 0x85 is not sent for a non-canonical GUID spelling of the right client (finding above) |
| MQTT CONNACK 0x84 | yes | Unsupported version | Matches by reading. `MQTT.md:43` gives the 3.1.1 return codes for 0x86, 0x87 and 0x85 only; 0x84 (1) and 0x88 (3) are missing |
| MQTT DISCONNECT 0x00 Normal | yes | Never: a Normal end is always the client's own DISCONNECT, by which time `DisconnectAsync` returns early | **Documented, never sent** (harmless; the row could say "not sent by the gateway") |
| MQTT 0x8B Shutting down | yes | `MqttConnection.CloseTransportAsync` and `MqttGateway.StopAsync:82-85` | Matches; loopback test |
| MQTT 0x95 Packet too large | yes | Only for a payload over 64 KB inside a packet under 65 KB; a bigger packet is cut with no DISCONNECT | Matches the doc (`MQTT.md:53-54`), but a client rarely sees 0x95 |
| MQTT 0x83 Server error | yes | `Error` | Matches by reading |
| MQTT 0x8E Session taken over | yes | Gateway for another host's connection; MQTTnet itself for a same-host takeover | Matches; E2E `MQTT_04`. The same-host case is where the P1 finding lives |
| MQTT 0x87 Not authorized | yes | `ClientAccessHandlers` through `MqttConnection` | Matches; E2E `MQTT_03` |
| MQTT 0x96 Rate too high | yes | `MessageBudget` | Matches by reading; no test over MQTT (#655) |
| MQTT 0x8D Keep alive timeout | **no** | MQTTnet's keep-alive monitor, on its own | **Sent, undocumented**: add a row ("reconnect on the schedule") |
| MQTT PUBACK 0x87, SUBACK 0x87 | yes | `MqttGateway.cs:159, 175` | Matches; QoS 0 publishes to a wrong topic are dropped with a log line only |
| MQTT DISCONNECT with `reason=updating` (client) | yes | `MqttDisconnectClassifier` | Matches; E2E `MQTT_05` |
| `sys.ping`, `sys.pong`, `sys.ack`, `sys.ota.update`, `sys.ota.status`, `capabilities`, `state.report` | yes | Gateway handlers for pong, ack and OTA status; management ingestion for the last two | Matches, except that the gateway matches the `sys.*` names case-sensitively (finding above) |

Other drift:

- `Tests/Conformance/README.md:14` says "A future binding (MQTT, HTTPS) adds its own checklist and its own scenarios". MQTT now has a binding and a checklist, with no scenarios.
- `Docs/Client.Protocol.md:264-265` ("see that binding's checklist") links the WebSocket checklist only, not `Client.Protocol.MQTT.md#5-conformance-checklist`. `Docs/Client.Protocol.WebSocket.md:87-88` says the two checklists are "everything the conformance suite checks"; the suite checks less (finding above).
- `Docs/Client.Protocol.MQTT.md:28` ("The gateway keeps no session between connections, whatever is asked") is not true during a takeover (P1 finding). `:29` ("The gateway also probes the TCP connection on its own") is true only with no proxy in front. `:30` ("a will message goes nowhere") is not true for a will on the up topic.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Gateway.MQTT.Host/Infrastructure/GuardedTcpServerAdapter.cs:100-102`: the comment promises detection of a vanished peer; behind Traefik the probe reaches Traefik.
- `Wbskt.Infrastructure/Security/ClientTokenCutoffs.cs` says the cutoff "is what makes revocation hold regardless"; it holds for new connections only (finding above).
- Tests that prove less than their names say: `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/Gateway/ClientConnectionTests.cs:34-41, 56` (the socket mock is always `Open`, so the 1001 mapping passes while the real socket is aborted); `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/Gateway/Mqtt/MqttGatewayTests.cs` (every connect uses `WithCleanSession()` and the canonical lower-case client id, so neither the session-reuse nor the GUID-spelling path is exercised; no will, no keep-alive 0, no unsubscribe, no throttle over MQTT).
- `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/Architecture/GatewayCoreBoundaryTests.cs` covers both hosts and holds: Gateway.Core references no transport assembly. It checks one direction only. Nothing checks that a host goes through `ClientSessions` for admission and presence (today both do: `SocketHandler` and `MqttGateway` call `AdmitAsync` and `RunAsync`), so a third binding could publish presence itself without a test failing. Not a finding; worth one more assertion when a third binding is added.
- `Docs/Device.Protocol.md` and `Docs/Device.Protocol.WebSocket.md` are three-line "Moved" stubs that point at the Client.Protocol pages. They are not diverging copies.

The two hosts compared (the transport seam): admission, the size check, the message budget, the allowance, supersede, revoke and presence all sit in Gateway.Core and behave the same. The differences found are the ones in the findings: shutdown close (MQTT right, WebSocket wrong), liveness (WebSocket enforces it, MQTT leaves it to the client), takeover (WebSocket through `ConnectionManager`, MQTT also through MQTTnet's own session handling), and the client id spelling. Binary WebSocket frames are skipped before the budget is charged (`SocketHandler.cs`), so they cost the gateway a read but never count; small.

### Client libraries

- wbskt.dotnet/README.md:15-17: the quick start begins with an empty line where `using Wbskt;` should be, so pasted into a new `Program.cs` it fails with CS0246. This README is the nuget.org page. The site's copy has the line (sdks.html:32). The README also has no sample for declaring commands, which needs the unobvious `new ClientCapabilities("", "", "", commands)` (conformance/Wbskt.Conformance.Harness/Program.cs:51).
- Wbskt.Arduino/README.md:271: "`enableOta(version)` ... reports `version` as the `firmware` state". The state variable was removed in e4d9a6d; the version now goes as `firmwareVersion` in capabilities (README.md:209 says so correctly).
- Wbskt.Arduino/README.md:327-328: "Commands sent while the board is offline are not queued by the server." They are, for up to 24 hours (Client.Protocol.md:175-177), which is why the library checks `expiresAt`.
- Wbskt.Arduino/README.md:365-367: calls `sys.ota.update` and `sys.ota.status` "this library's proposal ... the server side is wbskt/Wbskt#510". They are in the protocol (Client.Protocol.md section 4.4) and the management API (`POST {clientRef}/ota`, Docs/API.Endpoints.md:389). README.md:176-178 ("the console doesn't send this command yet") is still true: the console has no OTA screen.
- Wbskt.Arduino/README.md: nothing says `send` and `reportState` must be called from the task that runs `wbskt.loop()`; the outbox and socket are not locked, so a sketch that sends from its own FreeRTOS task races them. Nothing says a sketch that turned on the loop watchdog (`enableLoopWDT`) will be reset during the 20 to 60 s OTA download that blocks `loop()` (README.md:195-196).
- Wbskt/Docs/SDK.Releasing.md:10: the ESP32 row says "Workflow: none: the registry polls tags" and names only the Arduino Library Manager. Wbskt.Arduino now has `release.yml`, which publishes to PlatformIO with a stored `PLATFORMIO_AUTH_TOKEN` in a `platformio` environment (Wbskt.Arduino/.github/workflows/release.yml:28-41). The one-time setup list (SDK.Releasing.md:15-28) has no step for that token or environment.
- Wbskt.Python/README.md:95: "`send` never blocks on the network". True, but once the burst of 50 is spent a connected `send` waits for the pacer (client.py:168-175), so a loop of 200 sends takes about 19 s; the ESP32 library queues instead (README.md:98-99 there). The README should say which.
- Wbskt.Python/README.md:99-107: the options table omits `firmware_version` and `reconnect_policy` (client.py:72-73).
- Wbskt.Python/src/wbskt/client.py:96: comment about Python 3.9, which 859513f dropped.
- Wbskt/Tests/Conformance/README.md:103-115: the sentence "its own CI runs this suite against that build:" ends in a colon and is followed by an unrelated paragraph; the command block it introduces is eight lines below.
- wbskt.dotnet/tests/Wbskt.Tests: no test constructs `WbsktClient` or `SocketClient`; the 28 tests cover helpers only, so the lifecycle-handler, size-limit and registration-429 gaps above have no unit test and no conformance scenario. tests/Wbskt.Tests/ProtocolRulesTests.cs:30-45 tests `ClientMessageRules.FindViolation` with a payload, a path the SDK never takes.
- Wbskt/Docs/Production.Readiness.Plan.md:601, 610: still describes `Clients/Wbskt.Client.Sdk`. It is a dated plan, so this is noted, not a finding. No other reference to `Clients/python` or `Clients/Wbskt.Client.Sdk` remains in the backend: `Wbskt.slnx`, the workflows, `README.md` and `Tests/Conformance` all name the new repositories.

### Workflow engine

- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Architecture.md:299` says "Waiting branches need nothing" after a restart. A branch whose bookmark was already claimed is `Waiting` with nothing to wake it (first finding).
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Design.Patterns.md:113` says the side effect of a parking step, "send the command, start the child", runs in `AfterParked` so a fast reply always finds the bookmark. `SubWorkflowNodeExecutor.cs:105` starts the child in the first visit, before the park; only the command step uses `AfterParked` (`CommandNodeExecutor.cs:113`).
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Design.Patterns.md:69` says a claim marked Failed can be reclaimed, so a crash mid-dispatch does not lose the event. A crash leaves the claim `Pending`, which is never reclaimed (`BookmarkResumer.cs:69-84`, `TriggerDispatcher.cs:430-432`).
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Features.md:206-207` and `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Architecture.md:313` say the reaper fails a stuck run. `RunReaper.cs:33-36` requests cancellation and finalizes, and `RunFinalizer.cs:124-137` ends such a run `Cancelled`. `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/HostedServices/RunReaperTests.cs` does not assert the final status either way.
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Features.md:249` and the comment at `Wbskt.Workflow.Abstraction/Models/Nodes/NodeKinds.cs:66` say a sub-workflow child spends its own credit budget. `Wbskt.Workflow/Runtime/RunStarter.cs:92-94` and `Databases/Wbskt.Database/StoredProcedures/Run_Start.sql` charge a child against its root run's budget. The credits lane owns which one is right.
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Features.md:329` gives "Active runs per workspace: 200". Architecture.md section 4.3 and `Run_Start` (error 50026) apply the plan's ceiling per tenant (20, 100 or 200 by plan).
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Features.md:349` says message text, URLs and payloads in action fields are "used as written". They are templated: the config records carry `[Templated]` on `message`, `url`, `body`, `payload`, `subject` and `to`, and Features.md's own templating section says so.
- `Wbskt.Workflow.Abstraction/Models/Nodes/NodeKinds.cs:16-17` says the five trigger kinds share `PassthroughTriggerExecutor`. Four do; the manual trigger has its own `ManualTriggerExecutor` since this range (`Wbskt.Workflow/Extensions/WorkflowServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:149-153`).
- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Architecture.md` section 8 (workers) has no row for `TriggerOutcomeFlusher`, and section 4.1 (inbound adapters) has no row for the test-run endpoint (`InboundTestRunController`), both added in this range.
- `Docs/API.Endpoints.md:462` lists the 409 on `POST {workflowRef}/runs` as "dropped by the concurrency policy". A manual trigger is always `AllowParallel`, so that 409 is only ever a plan refusal (fourth finding).
- `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/ManagementHost/WorkflowTestRunServiceTests.cs:80` pins `TEST_RUN_TRIGGER_REQUIRED` for the unnamed case but has no case for a definition with no trigger, which gets the same "more than one trigger" message.
- No test under `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests` covers recovery of a `Waiting` branch whose bookmark is gone, a `Pending` claim met on redelivery, or a finalizer step that throws after the terminal status is set (searched by the codes and type names; the integration test project was not read).

### Database

- `Docs/Workflow.Engine/Architecture.md:118` names `TriggerDispatchOutcome_Record`; the procedure is `TriggerDispatchOutcome_RecordMany` since 4dd985b.
- `Docs/Billing.md:21,204-214` says a finished run is kept "with its branches and history" for the plan's days; routine history goes at 30 days (second finding above).
- `Databases/Wbskt.Database/Functions/TenantCreditGrantBalance.sql:1` still says "not drawn by a settled month"; month settlement was removed in this range (the pre-deployment script drops `TenantCreditGrants_Settle` and `TenantCreditGrantSettlements`).
- `Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/StoredProcedures/User_Delete.sql:34-35` says accepting an invitation does not lock the tenant row; since fb7ca4b it does when the plan has a member limit (`TenantInvitation_Accept.sql:53`).
- `Databases/Wbskt.Database/StoredProcedures/Bookmark_DeleteOrphans.sql:10` lists the status `'Faulted'`, and the comments at `Run_SetTerminal.sql:10` and `Run_UpdateStatus.sql:11` mention it; `CK_Runs_Status` (`Tables/Runs.sql:37-39`) does not allow that status.
- `Docs/AccessControl.md:166` describes `dbo.User_SetActive` as the operator's way to disable an account; nothing but one test calls it and no runbook shows how.
- `Databases/Wbskt.Database/Tables/Clients.sql:8` documents `Status` as 0, 1, 2; `ClientStatus` also has `Rejected = 3` (`Clients/Wbskt.Protocol/ClientAuth.cs:7-13`), which `Workspace_Retire.sql:38` relies on.

### Console and site

- `GEMINI.md` (console) names things that were renamed or removed: `ConsoleShell`, `ConsoleHeader`, `ExplorerPanel`, `TabHost`, `features/console/layout`, `features/workspaces`, `features/console` (now `WorkspaceShell`, `WorkspaceHeader`, `Explorer`, `Editor`, `features/dashboard`, `features/workspace`); the mixin file `_auth.scss` with `auth-page`, `auth-form-panel` and `$auth-breakpoint` (`src/styles/mixins` holds `_controls`, `_editor`, `_explorer`, `_index`, `_surfaces`); the `auth-brand-panel` layout component; `policy-pin-panel`, `policy-capacity-panel`, `policy-queues-panel` (now `policy-enrollment-panel`, `policy-settings-panel`, `policy-templates-panel`, `policy-header`); `console/utils/format.ts` (now `core/utils/time-format.util.ts`); `toClientState()` in `models/client.model.ts` (now `clients/utils/client-state.util.ts`); `console/components/policy-form-modal/` (now `policies/dialogs/policy-form-modal/`).
- `GEMINI.md` backend-gap statements that are no longer true: "No regenerate-PIN endpoint; the button surfaces a 'not supported yet' notice" (the console calls `ROTATE_PIN`); "`/clients/{ref}/comms` has no direction field" (the console passes `direction`); "`EventLogResponse` has no actor" (it has `userRefId`); "`WorkspaceResponse` is `(RefId, Name, Description, CreatedAt)`" (it also has `TenantRefId`).
- `GEMINI.md` says `WorkspaceDataService` is "the only place the console fetches policies and clients". `PolicyClientsService` (its own pager on `GET /clients?policyRefId=`), `ClientPageService` and `ClientService.clientCommands` also fetch.
- `GEMINI.md` describes the status bar's workspace message rate (`OnWorkspaceMessageRateEvent`, `GET .../stats/messages`, the stale rule) as if built. The console has no listener and no call; it is part of #148.
- `GEMINI.md` says `policy-tab` keys its effect on the tab id; `policy-tab.ts:50` reads the whole `tab()` (see the finding).
- Code comments that contradict the code or the API: `workflow.service.ts:65` ("The list doesn't return it"); `workflow-run.service.ts:19` and `workflow-runs.ts:16-18` ("no workspace-wide run endpoint"; `GET /workspaces/{ref}/runs` exists); `workflow-api.model.ts:125-126` (run statuses "Completed", "Faulted"; summary said to lack trigger and credits); the deprecate comment in `workflow-api.service.ts` ("the API has no matching 'enable'", with `reinstate` directly below it); `client-page.service.ts` on `sendCommand` ("Commands are delivered live or not at all"; the server queues for an offline client and answers `status: "queued"`); `workflow.service.ts` ("has no draft state"); `workspace-api.model.ts:26` ("It carries no owner flag"); `members.service.ts:185` ("The workspace list doesn't say which account a workspace belongs to").
- Site: the docs' "All 24 workflow nodes" against the console palette's 22 entries (Client libraries lane owns the docs pages).
- Tests: the console has no spec files (by guideline), so no test contradicts the code. Nothing in the site has tests.

There are six host services (`deploy/scripts/deploy.sh:28-29`).

- `deploy/README.md:495-496` (Scaling) omits `GATEWAY_MQTT_REPLICAS` and `CLIENT_AUTH_REPLICAS`; `:778` (the stop command before a restore) omits gateway-mqtt, which would stay connected during the restore.

- `deploy/docker/Dockerfile:2` and `:19` "all 4 hosts"; `deploy/observability/prometheus.yml:1` "the four hosts"; `deploy/observability/otel-collector.yaml:3` "four services"; `deploy/scripts/deploy.sh:358` "the five hosts".
- `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/Architecture/PublicRoutingTests.cs:23-33` checks that the router label contains the exclusion text, not that a request is refused (see the `/api/internal` finding).
- `deploy/observability/alerts.yml:289-290` refers to a container memory limit that compose does not set.
- `.github/workflows/nightly-stress.yml:121` says "200 clients register under a fresh policy"; under the Free plan 20 do.

## Dead or obsolete code

### Auth and management

- `Wbskt.Infrastructure/Security/WbsktClaims.cs:44-47, 76, 86-105`: readers for the legacy `type` and `uref` claims, kept "for one token lifetime" after the claim rename. Tokens from before it expired long ago. `Docs/API.Endpoints.md:115` still says legacy tokens are accepted; remove both together (fits #333).
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Management.Host/Hosting/HostRole.cs:27-29, 50-53`: the `"Devices"` spelling of the ClientAuth role. Both compose files set `Host__Role: ClientAuth`, and the other Device shims went in `3f2f416`.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Management.Host/Services/EventLogService.cs:239, 252-258`: `"Device"` accepted as an event-log source name for "saved filters and links". The console saves no such filters and the product has not launched; fits #333.
- Console `src/app/core/signalr/signalr-methods.enum.ts:39`: `OnPolicyCreated` is defined and never used (see the console finding, which would use it).

### Credits and usage

- `Wbskt.Workflow/Providers/RunProvider.cs:16-36` (`IRunProvider.CreateAsync`) and `Databases/Wbskt.Database/StoredProcedures/Run_Create.sql`: no production caller; only integration tests seed runs with it. It creates a run with no start charge, no plan check, no root branch and no `IsTest`, so it is also the one way to make an uncounted run if anything ever calls it. Move the seeding to a test helper and drop both.
- `Wbskt.Workflow/Providers/RunCountersProvider.cs:15-24` (`GetByRunIdAsync`), `Databases/Wbskt.Database/StoredProcedures/RunCounters_GetBy_RunId.sql` and `RunCountersRow`: read only by tests; the row does not carry `PostedCredits`, `ChildCreditsConsumed`, `BudgetRunId` or `BudgetCeiling`.
- `Tests/Wbskt.Host.Tests/Runtime/DefaultCreditCostCalculatorTests.cs`: obsolete (see above); `CreditCostCalculatorTests.cs:68` already covers the default.

### Billing and pricing

- Wbskt/Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Billing/DodoOptions.cs:48: the third fallback `DODO_WEBHOOK_SECRET` is in no doc, compose file or `.env.example` (they name `DODO_PAYMENTS_WEBHOOK_KEY`); it is either a leftover or an undocumented setting.
- Wbskt/Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Billing/StandardWebhookSignature.cs:61-66: `Sign` is called only from tests ("for tests and local replays"); it ships in the host for no caller.
- Wbskt/Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/Billing/TenantBillingSync.cs:196: `MarkDuplicateSubscriptionAsync` returns whether it marked now and the result is discarded, so the error log and the cancel run again on every delivery until the duplicate stops billing. Harmless, but the return value has no reader.
- Wbskt/Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/StoredProcedures/TenantPlan_Set.sql:15-16: the defaults `@PlanSource = N'Operator'` and `@PlanEndsAt = NULL` are never relied on (TenantPlanProvider.cs:41 always passes both); a default that makes an omitted source an operator's plan is the wrong way round if a caller ever does omit it.
- No unused billing procedure was found: each of the 24 is called from `TenantBillingProvider` or `TenantPlanProvider`.

### Tenants and access

- `UserPermissionsChangedEvent` (Events/Wbskt.Events/Auth/UserPermissionsChangedEvent.cs:8) and `RolePermissionsChangedEvent` (Events/Wbskt.Events/Auth/RolePermissionsChangedEvent.cs:7) are published from Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/TenantAnnouncer.cs:20, AccountDeletionService.cs:151 and AccessControlService.cs:79, 328, 467, and consumed nowhere: a search of every host and library finds only the publishers and their unit tests. The cache invalidation they were meant for is done by `WorkspaceAccessChanges`.
- `dbo.User_SetActive` (Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/StoredProcedures/User_SetActive.sql) has no caller in any host; only Tests/Wbskt.Workflow.Engine.Host.IntegrationTests/AuthDatabase/TenantAdministratorGuardTests.cs:104 and 121 execute it. Docs/Auth.Host.E2E.Scenarios.md:187 says account deactivation was retired from the API. Either drop it (project and pre-deployment script) or document it as an operator-only tool.
- Previous review item L8 is still there with no open issue in the dump: `new TenantAnnouncer(...)` inside constructors at Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Services/AccountDeletionService.cs:46, TenantService.cs:54 and AccessControlService.cs:34. L9 (`POST /api/auth/account/delete`) is now written into Coding.Conventions.md as the accepted exception, so it is closed.

### Gateways

- The 1008 compatibility story: `Docs/Client.Protocol.WebSocket.md:68`, the `1008` case in `Tests/Conformance/test_conformance.py:148-149` and its comment. No gateway that sends 1008 exists any more, and the product has not launched, so there is no older gateway for an SDK to meet. Keep the test case only if "any unknown policy close reconnects" is the rule being checked, and say that.
- `Wbskt.Gateway.Core/Handlers/ClientCommandHandler.cs:93-103`: the branch for "a command with no id predates the queue". Every sender now sets a command id; the branch and the nullable `CommandId` on `ClientCommandEvent` can go together.
- `Wbskt.Gateway.Core/Infrastructure/IClientConnection.cs:43-45` and the `PingPayload` field "which older servers read": compatibility with SDKs from before the ping id. All three SDKs in the sibling repos send the id; check with the client-libraries lane before removing.
- `Docs/Device.Protocol.md`, `Docs/Device.Protocol.WebSocket.md`: redirect stubs. Fine to keep for one release; delete once nothing links to them (the sibling SDK repos' READMEs are the likely linkers; not checked).
- `Clients/Wbskt.Protocol` is `IsPackable=false` and both `Clients/Wbskt.Simulator` and `Tests/Wbskt.LoadTester` use the published `Wbskt` 0.9.0-beta.1 package, the same version `wbskt.dotnet/Directory.Build.props` builds. Nothing dead here; noted because the 2 Nov H4 asked for it.

### Client libraries

- wbskt.dotnet/src/Wbskt/Protocol/ClientMessageRules.cs:14, 22, 37-40, 50-63, 70: `MaxPayloadChars`, `FindViolation`, the payload branch of `Check`, `CheckPlatformCommand` and `ClientMessageRule.PayloadSize` are server-side rules copied from the platform's `Wbskt.Protocol`; the SDK calls only `Check(type, null)`. The class comment (lines 5-8) still describes "the console, a message template, or a workflow step".
- wbskt.dotnet/src/Wbskt/Protocol/Payloads.cs:9-11 (`PingPayload`), ClientAuth.cs:34 (`ClientErrorCodes.NotRegistered`), MessageTypes.cs:32 (`OtaStatus`), WebSocketBinding.cs:16 (`UpdatingCloseCode`), WebSocketMessageReader.cs:41 (`MaxMessageBytes` property), ProtocolMessage.cs:11 and 29-32 (`CommandId`, `ExpiresAt`, never set by the SDK, which reads raw JSON): no caller in src/.
- wbskt.dotnet/src/Wbskt/Internal/OutboundBuffer.cs:20: `DroppedCount` is counted and never surfaced. The ESP32 library exposes `droppedMessages()`; .NET exposes only `PendingMessageCount` and Python neither.
- wbskt.dotnet/src/Wbskt/WbsktClient.cs:102, 137-141: a try block whose only catch is `catch (Exception) { throw; }`.
- Wbskt.Python/src/wbskt/protocol.py:23: `API_PREFIX` is used only to build the two paths on the next lines; harmless. `protocol.REGISTERED` (line 20) has no reader in src/.

### Workflow engine

- `Databases/Wbskt.Database/StoredProcedures/Bookmark_DeleteOrphans.sql:10` lists a run status `'Faulted'`. No run status of that name exists in `Wbskt.Workflow.Abstraction/Entities` or anywhere else in the C#.
- `Wbskt.Workflow.Abstraction/Runtime/ISubWorkflowCompletionHook.cs:5` (the three-argument `OnRunCompletedAsync`) and its implementation at `Wbskt.Workflow/Runtime/SubWorkflowCompletionHook.cs:9-10` have no caller outside `SubWorkflowCompletionHookTests`; `RunFinalizer.cs:118` calls the four-argument overload.
- `Hosts/Wbskt.Management.Host/Services/Workflow/WorkflowTestRunService.cs:91` sets `CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow` on the row passed to `InsertTestCopyAsync`. `WorkflowDefinition_InsertTestCopy.sql` does not insert that column (the table default applies), so the value is unused, and it is the only `DateTime.UtcNow` on this path where the convention asks for `TimeProvider`.
- Console: `method: WEBHOOK_METHOD` in the webhook trigger's saved config (see the finding above) is a field with no reader.

- `dbo.WorkflowDefinition_FindBy_RefId` (`Databases/Wbskt.Database/StoredProcedures/WorkflowDefinition_FindBy_RefId.sql`): no caller anywhere.
- `dbo.User_SetActive` (`Databases/Wbskt.Database.Auth/StoredProcedures/User_SetActive.sql`): called only by one integration test.

### Infra and CI

- `.github/workflows/build-images.yml:672`, `:688`, `:709`, `:720`: conditions that test for a pull request inside jobs that never run on one (`:642`, `:698`).
- `nuget.config`: only disables three private feeds from one developer's machine; it has no `<clear/>` and no explicit nuget.org source, so it does nothing in CI and does not stop a feed added on a build machine from being used.
- `deploy/docker/Dockerfile:39` removes `rabbitmq.json`, `serilog.json`, `connectionstrings.json` and `workflow-engine.json` from the image but not `email.json`, which `Hosts/Wbskt.Auth.Host/Wbskt.Auth.Host.csproj:46` copies in. Harmless (the environment overrides it); it belongs on the same list.

- The provisioned dashboard `deploy/observability/grafana/provisioning/dashboards/wbskt/overview.json` has no panel for billing webhooks, repaired credit grants, mail, `wbskt_socket_messages_dropped_total` (the throttle) or `wbskt_event_queue_dropped_total`; the last has no alert either. Not dead code, but these are emitted and shown nowhere.
