| Finding | Status on master | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| H2 requeue loop, presence never repaired | Fixed (#539 closed). Two residues are new findings above | Loop: `returnedByHostId` in `ClientCommandQueue.cs:78-88`. Lost disconnect: `SocketHostPresenceSnapshotHandler` and `Client_ReconcilePresenceBy_HostId`. Residues: the snapshot does not repair "shown offline but connected"; the same-host hand-back can wait |
| M1 trigger outcome writes per message | Fixed (#542 closed) for the outcome log | `Wbskt.Workflow/Runtime/TriggerDispatcher.cs:238` only buffers; `TriggerOutcomeBuffer` and `TriggerOutcomeFlusher` write in batches. `ClientHoldRecorder.cs:53` still awaits one hold-state write per message per hold trigger; that is for the workflow lane to judge |
| M2 OTA looks like an outage | Fixed (#543 closed) | 4000 Updating, MQTT `reason=updating`, `UpdatingUntil`; E2E `MQTT_05` |
| M3 capability reports in consumption order | Fixed (#544 closed) | `ClientCapabilities_Upsert.sql` compares `@ReportedAt`; `ClientMetadataIngestionHandler.cs:202-203` passes `message.CreatedAtUtc` |
| M4 command lifetime a caller convention | Fixed (#545 closed) | `ClientCommandSender` enforces `CommandLifetime.Max` |
| M5 one close code for three reasons | Fixed (#546 closed) | 4001 to 4003, `SupersedeMonitor`, `ClientCredentialsSharedEvent` |
| M6 deleting a client re-enrols it | Still open, filed: #547 (wbskt/Wbskt) | No change in `ClientAuthService`; decision pending |
| M7 conformance only meets a fake | Mostly fixed (#548 closed); residue unfiled | Fake now has the budget, 4001, 403 and 400; `test_live.py` runs nightly. Still missing: see the conformance finding above |
| M8 a broker blip ends sessions | Fixed (dd70bc8) | `InboundMessageDispatcher.cs:69-99`, `ClientSessions.AnnounceConnectedAsync`. It created the conditions for two new findings (presence after a long outage; a missed revoke) |
| M9 one SQL call per state variable | Fixed (#550 closed) | `ClientStateVariables_Report.sql` (one TVP call); `ClientMetadataIngestionHandler.cs:103` |

### Database: unused procedures, indexes and lock order

##### Unused procedures
Method: every `CREATE PROCEDURE` name in both projects (316) was searched as a quoted string in all non-test C#, then in tests, SQL, scripts and CI workflows. The reverse check (every procedure name quoted in C# exists in a project) found nothing missing. Parameters of the procedures changed in this range were compared with what the providers pass; no mismatch (`Branch_Upsert` and `Branch_InsertIfAbsent` add theirs through the shared helper `AddRowParameters`).

| Procedure | Database | State |
|---|---|---|
| `WorkflowDefinition_FindBy_RefId` | Wbskt | No caller. Delete the file and drop it in the pre-deployment script. |
| `User_SetActive` | Auth | Test-only caller. Keep with a comment if operators use it, else drop. |
| `ClientReadings_DeleteBefore` | Wbskt | Removed from the project in e475fee, not dropped on deploy. |
| `ClientStateVariable_Upsert` | Wbskt | Removed in 2e910ac, not dropped on deploy. |
| `TriggerDispatchOutcome_Record` | Wbskt | Removed in 4dd985b, not dropped on deploy. |
| `TenantCreditGrants_Draw`, `WorkflowRunSequence_Next` | Wbskt | Called only from other procedures, by design. Not unused. |

##### Index table
From code alone (no usage numbers, see #619). "FK" marks a foreign key column.

| Table | Indexes | What the queries ask for | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Clients` | PK `(Id)`, UQ `(RefId)`, `(PolicyId)`, `(WorkspaceId)` | By `RefId`; lists by `WorkspaceId` paged on `Id`; `COUNT ... Status = 1` by workspace and by policy; presence sweeps by `IsConnected`, `ConnectedHostId` | Presence sweeps scan. Counts look up every row for `Status`. See finding. |
| `ClientTraffic` | PK `(Id)`, `(WorkspaceId, Id DESC)` incl., `(ClientId, Id DESC)` filtered, UX `(MessageId, WorkspaceId)` filtered | Workspace and client feeds newest first by cursor; duplicate check by `MessageId` | Fits. `EventId` FK has no index, harmless: `Events` rows are never deleted. Retention delete is by `Id` range. |
| `EventLogs` | PK `(Id)`, `(EventId)`, `(CreatedAt)`, `(WorkspaceId, Id DESC)`, `(WorkspaceId, CreatedAt)` incl., `(ClientId, Id DESC)` filt., `(UserId, CreatedAt DESC)` filt., `(PolicyId)` filt., UX `(MessageId, WorkspaceId)` filt., `(WorkflowId)` filt. | Workspace list newest first with optional filters; counts in a time window; client comms; retention by `CreatedAt` | `(UserId, CreatedAt)` and `(WorkflowId)` have no reader; `(PolicyId)` nearly none. Ten indexes on an insert-heavy table. |
| `HistoryEvents` | PK `(RunId, HistoryEventId)`, `(HistoryEventId)`, `(Timestamp, Severity)` | By `RunId` after a cursor; delete by `HistoryEventId` joined to `Runs.CompletedAt` | `(Timestamp, Severity)` has no reader. The Info delete walks from the lowest id past every kept Warn and Error row on each call. |
| `Runs` | UQ `(RefId)` and 12 more: by `WorkflowRefId` (`Id`, `CreatedAt`, correlation key active, test active, `Number`), by `WorkflowDefinitionId` (`Id`, `CreatedAt`, `CompletedAt`, active), `(CompletedAt)`, `(ParentRunRefId)` active, UX `(TriggerStartKey)` | Lists by workflow or version newest first; active counts; retention by `CompletedAt`; start de-duplication | Each has a named reader. The `WorkflowRefId` and `WorkflowDefinitionId` families mirror each other; whether both are used is a question for the usage numbers. Thirteen indexes are paid on every run insert and status change. |
| `Branches` | UQ `(RefId)`, `(RunId)`, `(RunId, Status)`, `(Status)` filt. Active, `(UpdatedAt)` filt. Waiting | By `RefId`; by run; active claim; stranded waiting sweep | `(RunId)` duplicates the prefix of `(RunId, Status)`. |
| `Bookmarks` | UQ `(RefId)`, `(MatchKey)` incl., `(ExpiresAt)` filt., `(RunId)` | Wake by `MatchKey`; due by `ExpiresAt` with `READPAST`; delete by run | Fits. |
| `IdempotencyKeys` | UQ `(KeyValue)`, `(RunId)`, `(CreatedAt)` | Lookup by key; delete by run and by age | Fits. |
| `QueuedCommands` | PK `(Id)`, UQ `(CommandId)`, `(ClientId, Id)`, `(ExpiresAt)` | Drain by client in order; expiry take with `READPAST` | Fits. |
| `ClientReadings`, `ClientReadingsHourly` | PK `(ClientId, Name, SentAt or HourStart)`, `(ClientId, ReceivedAt or HourStart)` page-compressed | Buckets by client, name and time; compaction by client and cutoff | Fits. Rows of a deleted workspace never leave (first finding). |
| `RunCounters` | PK `(RunId)` only | By `RunId`; the 10 second posting sweep for rows with unposted credits | The key covers the FK. The posting sweep starts from `Runs` that are active or ended since the last post (`RunCredits_Post.sql:36-43`, served by the filtered active index and `IX_Runs_CompletedAt`) and joins by `RunId`, so it does not scan. |
| `WorkspaceCreditUsage` | PK `(WorkspaceId, Day)` | By workspace and month | Fits. |
| `WorkspaceMessageUsage` | PK `(WorkspaceId, Day)` | By workspace and month; all workspaces since month start, every minute | The every-minute read scans. See finding. |
| `TenantCreditGrants` | PK `(Id)`, UQ `(Source, SourceRef)`, `(TenantId, ExpiresAt)` incl. | Draw by tenant in expiry order; revoke and hold by source; expiring claim by `ExpiresAt` range across tenants | The expiring claim scans (small table). Lock order with the draw: see finding. |
| `TriggerRegistrations`, `ScheduledFires`, `PendingTriggerEvents` | By `TriggerKey` incl.; `(WorkflowDefinitionId)`; `(NextFireAt)`; run key and `(EnqueuedAt)` | Dispatch by key; due fires; queue per run key | Fits. |
| `MessageTemplates` | PK, UQ `(RefId)`, `(WorkspaceId)` | By workspace; `RegistrationPolicy_Delete` clears `PolicyId` | FK `PolicyId` has no index; the policy delete scans it. Small table. |
| Auth `UserRoles`, `GroupRoles`, `UserPermissions` | PK `(Id)`, one UQ starting with user or group | Permission resolution by user (fits); deletes by `WorkspaceId`, `RoleId`, `TenantId` | FKs `RoleId`, `TenantId`, `WorkspaceId` (and `PermissionId`) unindexed. See finding. |
| Auth `TenantMembers` | PK `(TenantId, UserId)` | By tenant; by user (`Tenant_GetForUser`, `User_Delete`) | FK `UserId` unindexed; by-user reads scan. |
| Auth `TenantInvitations` | PK, UQ `(RefId)`, UQ `(TokenHash)`, UX `(TenantId, Email)` pending | By token; pending by tenant and email; update by `RoleId`; update by `Email`; expiry delete by `ExpiresAt` | FKs `RoleId`, `InvitedByUserId`, `AcceptedByUserId` unindexed; the role, email and expiry statements scan. |
| Auth `RolePermissions`, `UserGroups`, `Groups`, `Workspaces` | PKs and names | By role, by user, by tenant | FKs `PermissionId`, `GroupId`, `ParentGroupId`, `OwnerUserId` unindexed. Small tables; matters only for `Group_Delete` and `User_Delete`. |
| Auth `RefreshTokens` | UQ `(TokenHash)`, `(UserId)` incl., `(SessionId)` incl., `(Expires)` | By hash; revoke by user or session; expiry delete | Fits. |
| Auth token tables (`PasswordResetTokens`, `EmailVerificationTokens`, `EmailChangeTokens`) | UQ `(TokenHash)`, UX `(UserId)` outstanding, `(ExpiresAt)` | By hash; one outstanding per user; expiry delete | FK `UserId` has only the filtered index, so a user delete scans consumed tokens. Small. |
| Auth `BillingNoticeMails` | PK, `(NextAttemptAt)` filtered on unsent | Take due with `UPDLOCK, READPAST, ROWLOCK`; purge sent ones by age | Dequeue fits. Purge scans, unbatched. FK `TenantId` unindexed (cascade on tenant delete scans). |
| Auth `BillingWebhookEvents` | PK, UQ `(Provider, EventId)` | Record by provider event id | Fits. Never pruned: already filed as #660. |

##### Lock-order table
Every transaction lives inside a procedure: no non-test C# opens a transaction, uses `TransactionScope` or sends command text, so none is held across a network call. Both databases run with read committed snapshot, so plain reads take no locks; the orders below are writes, hinted reads and application locks.

| Procedure | Order of locks | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Run_Start` | applock `trigger-start` → applock `test-run` → applock `plan-limit:runs:tenant` → applock `credit-cap:{ws}` → root `RunCounters` row → `WorkflowRunSequences` → `Runs` → `RunCounters` → `Branches` | One fixed order. Applocks always before rows. |
| `Run_Create` | `WorkflowRunSequences` → `Runs` → `RunCounters` | Same tail as `Run_Start`. |
| `RunCounters_TryCharge` | fast path: one `RunCounters` update. Slow path: applock `credit-cap:{ws}` → `RunCounters` and `Runs` (`UPDLOCK`) → `Runs` → `RunCounters` | Applock before rows, as `Run_Start`. |
| `RunCredits_Post` | `RunCounters` → `WorkspaceCreditUsage` (`MERGE HOLDLOCK`) → `TenantCreditGrants_Draw` | Every 10 s. |
| `TenantCreditGrants_Draw` | `TenantCreditMonths` (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `TenantCreditGrants` index `(TenantId, ExpiresAt)` (`UPDLOCK`) → `TenantCreditGrants` rows → `TenantCreditMonths` | Inverts with `TenantCreditGrant_Revoke` and `_SetHold` (row → same index key). Finding (b). |
| `Run_DeleteRetired` | `RunCounters` → `WorkspaceCreditUsage` → `TenantCreditGrants_Draw` → `HistoryEvents` → `Bookmarks` → `IdempotencyKeys` → `JoinContributions` → `JoinAggregators` → `RunCounters` → `Branches` → `Runs` → `WorkflowDefinitions` (test copies) | Same head as `RunCredits_Post`. Deletes children before `Runs`. Only finished runs, so it does not meet the engine's writes. |
| `ClientReadings_Compact` | `ClientReadings` → `ClientReadingsHourly` (`MERGE HOLDLOCK`); then outside the transaction `ClientReadingsHourly`, `ClientReadings`, `DeletedClientReadings` | Only writer besides the insert path. |
| `ClientTraffic_InsertBatch` | `ClientTraffic` → `WorkspaceMessageUsage` (`MERGE HOLDLOCK`) | Single order. |
| `Client_Create` | `RegistrationPolicies` row (`UPDLOCK`) → applock `plan-limit:clients:tenant` → `Clients` | |
| `Client_UpdateStatuses` | `Clients` rows (`UPDLOCK`) → `RegistrationPolicies` rows (`UPDLOCK`) → applock `plan-limit:clients:tenant` → `Clients` | Policy before applock, as `Client_Create`. Inverts with `Workspace_Retire` (policies → clients). Finding (c). |
| `Client_Delete` | `Clients` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `ClientStateVariables` → `ClientMessageTypes` → `ClientCapabilities` → `ClientTags` → `QueuedCommands` → `Clients` → `DeletedClientReadings` | Parent row first. |
| `Client_ExpirePresence`, `Client_ReconcilePresenceBy_HostId` | `Clients` (full scan with update locks), then `SocketHosts` | No transaction; blocks behind and in front of row writers. Finding on `Clients` indexes. |
| `RegistrationPolicy_Delete` | `RegistrationPolicies` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `Clients` range read (`HOLDLOCK`) → `MessageTemplates` → `RegistrationPolicies` | Its shared range lock does not block `Client_UpdateStatuses`' update locks. |
| `WorkflowDraft_Save` | `WorkflowDrafts` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → applock `workflow-draft-ceiling:{ws}` → `WorkflowDrafts` | |
| `WorkflowDefinition_Publish` | `WorkflowDrafts` row → `WorkflowDefinitions` range (`HOLDLOCK, UPDLOCK`) → applock `workflow-ceiling:{ws}` → `Integrations` (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `WorkflowDefinitions` → `WorkflowIntegrationRefs` → `WorkflowDrafts` | Drafts before definitions everywhere. |
| `WorkflowDefinition_Delete` | `WorkflowDrafts` row → `WorkflowDefinitions` range → `WorkflowDeletions` → `TriggerRegistrations` → `ScheduledFires` → `PendingTriggerEvents` → `TriggerDispatchOutcomes` → `WorkflowDrafts` → `WorkflowDefinitions` | Consistent with Save and Publish. Inverts with `Workspace_Retire`. Finding (c). |
| `Workspace_Retire` | `RegistrationPolicies` → `Clients` → `TriggerRegistrations` → `ScheduledFires` → `PendingTriggerEvents` → `TriggerDispatchOutcomes` → `WorkflowDefinitions` → `WorkflowIntegrationRefs` → `Integrations` → `WorkflowDrafts` → `WorkspaceAccounts` | Registrations before drafts, policies before clients: the opposite of the two above. Only for a deleted workspace. |
| `Integration_Delete` | `Integrations` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `WorkflowIntegrationRefs` → `Integrations` | |
| `JoinAggregator_Contribute` | `JoinAggregators` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `JoinContributions` → `JoinAggregators` | |
| `PendingTriggerEvent_Enqueue`, `_DequeueNext` | applock per run key → `PendingTriggerEvents` (`UPDLOCK, READPAST` on dequeue) | |
| Dequeues: `Bookmark_ClaimDue`, `QueuedCommand_TakeExpired`, `ScheduledFire_LeaseDue`, `ClientHoldState_LeaseDue`, `ClientPresenceCheck_LeaseDue`, Auth `BillingNoticeMails_Take` | one table each | All use `READPAST`. |
| Auth `TenantInvitation_Accept` | `TenantInvitations` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `Tenants` row (`UPDLOCK`, only with a member limit) → `TenantInvitations` → `TenantMembers` → `UserRoles` → `WorkspaceMembers` | The only procedure that takes the tenant row second. Finding (a). |
| Auth `Role_Delete` | `Tenants` row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `RolePermissions` → `UserRoles` → `GroupRoles` → `TenantInvitations` (scan) → `Roles` | Tenant first. |
| Auth `TenantMember_Remove`, `TenantMember_Leave` | `Tenants` row → `TenantMembers` (`HOLDLOCK`) → `WorkspaceMembers` → `Workspaces` → `UserRoles` → `UserPermissions` → `UserGroups` → `WorkspaceMembers` → `TenantInvitations` (by tenant and email, which the pending index can seek) → `TenantMembers` | Tenant first. |
| Auth `User_Delete` | `Tenants` rows of every tenant of the user → `TenantMembers` (`HOLDLOCK`) → `TenantMember_Remove` → `Workspace_Delete` → assignment tables → `Groups` → `TenantInvitations` → `Roles` → `Tenants` → tokens → `TenantInvitations` (scan by `Email`) → `Users` | Tenant first. Long: its scans of unindexed tables hold locks to the end. Read lightly. |
| Auth `Workspace_Delete` | `UserRoles` → `GroupRoles` → `UserPermissions` → `Workspaces` (FK checks scan the three again) → `DeletedWorkspaces` → `OutboxMessages` | Takes no tenant lock. Two at once can deadlock on the scans. Finding on auth indexes. |
| Auth `Workspace_Create` | `Tenants` row (`UPDLOCK`) → `Workspaces` → `WorkspaceMembers` → `UserRoles` | Tenant first. |
| Auth `Tenant_Create` | `Tenants` → `Roles` → `RolePermissions` → `TenantMembers` → `UserRoles` → `Workspaces` → `WorkspaceMembers` | New rows only. |
| Auth `EmailChange_Confirm`, `EmailChange_Revert`, `PasswordResetToken_Consume`, `EmailVerificationToken_Consume` | token row (`UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK`) → `Users` → `RefreshTokens` | Same order in all four. `User_ChangePassword` is `Users` → `RefreshTokens` too. |
| Auth `BillingNotice_Record`, `BillingWebhookEvent_Record`, `TenantCreditPurchase_Record`, `TenantPlan_Set` | one business table → `BillingNoticeMails` or `OutboxMessages` | Outbox always last. |

### Client libraries: the three libraries side by side

Library comparison (checks 1 to 7 of the lane brief). "Same" means the three agree with the protocol documents.

| Behaviour | .NET (wbskt.dotnet) | Python | ESP32 (Wbskt.Arduino) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Wait after a drop | 0-10 s, noticed by a 1 s poll (Internal/ReconnectPolicy.cs:11, 29-34; WbsktClient.cs:19, 194-217) | 0-10 s (reconnect.py:12, 24-30) | 0-10 s (wbskt/Reconnect.h:12; WbsktClient.cpp:352-356) |
| After 1001 restarting | 0-30 s | 0-30 s | 0-10 s: the WebSockets library hides close codes. Documented (README.md:305-307; Client.Protocol.WebSocket.md:69-70) |
| After 4003 throttled | 30-60 s (ReconnectPolicy.cs:17, 32) | 30-60 s (reconnect.py:15, 28-29) | 0-10 s, same documented reason; it paces itself so should not be throttled |
| After 4002 revoked | drops the token (WbsktClient.cs:206-210) | drops the token (client.py:194-196) | keeps it until an upgrade answers 401; documented |
| After 4001 superseded | `OnSuperseded` event, class only | log warning (client.py:197-199) | nothing |
| n-th failure in a row | 0-min(60 s, 2 s x 2^n), reset on connect (ReconnectPolicy.cs:37-42; WbsktClient.cs:222) | same (reconnect.py:32-34; client.py:193) | same (Reconnect.cpp:9-15; WbsktClient.cpp:310) |
| 429 on login | Retry-After + 0-2 s; no header: exponential | Retry-After + 0-2 s; no header: 0-2 s flat | as Python (WbsktClient.cpp:240-243) |
| 429 on registration | not honoured (finding) | honoured | honoured |
| 503 on login or registration | ordinary failure, `Retry-After` not read | same | same. The protocol asks for `Retry-After` only on 429, so this is consistent, not a finding |
| 401 on the upgrade | drops the token (SocketClient.cs:68-72; WbsktClient.cs:131-135) | drops the token (client.py:237-240) | drops it; with WebSockets before 2.7.2, after three unexplained failures (WbsktClient.cpp:324-331) |
| 403 `CLIENT_REVOKED`, 503 on the upgrade | keeps the token | keeps the token (conformance run) | keeps the token (conformance run) |
| `CLIENT_UNAUTHORIZED` at login | clears credentials, registers again (WbsktClient.cs:116-123) | same (client.py:342-345) | same (WbsktClient.cpp:283-286). Product question still open as #547 |
| No credentials and no PIN | `StartAsync` throws on the first attempt only; later silent retry (finding) | stops; `wait_closed()` raises | logs and stops (WbsktClient.cpp:173-177) |
| Offline buffer | 1,000 messages, oldest dropped (ClientConfig.cs:14; Internal/OutboundBuffer.cs:32-36) | 1,000 messages, oldest dropped (client.py:35, 88) | 16 KB of bytes, oldest dropped (WbsktClient.h:61; wbskt/Outbox.cpp:38-41). Documented on the site (sdks.html:54) |
| Flush pacing | 8 a second after 50, token bucket, backlog included (WbsktClient.cs:13, 301-314) | same (client.py:89, 270-277) | same (WbsktClient.cpp:63, 576-585) |
| Over the rate while connected | the caller waits | the caller waits | queued, `send` returns at once |
| Message over 64 KB | not refused (finding) | `ValueError` | `false` |
| Credentials at rest | JSON file, in place, umask mode (finding) | JSON file, temp + 0600 + rename | NVS namespace `wbskt`, keys `ref` and `secret`, plain NVS (WbsktClient.cpp:19-21, 263-266) |
| Access token | memory only (Internal/ClientToken.cs) | memory only | memory only |
| Closing on stop | aborts the socket, no close frame (SocketClient.cs:238-262) | close 1000 (client.py:118-123) | close 1000; 4000 before an OTA download (WbsktClient.cpp:493) |
| Handler exceptions | message handler isolated; `OnConnected`, `OnSuperseded` not (finding) | `on_message`, `on_connected` isolated; `on_disconnected` not | no exceptions on the build |
| Send from another thread | safe (buffer lock, flush lock, send lock) | sync client safe from other threads, hangs from a handler (finding); async client is single-loop | not safe off the loop task, undocumented |

1. Behaviour alike: see the table. The 2 Nov H1 is fixed in all three: pacing is in the protocol (Client.Protocol.md:114-118), the fake enforces the gateway's budget (Tests/Conformance/fake_wbskt.py:123-140, 205-211), and `test_paces_a_large_offline_backlog_under_the_gateway_budget` passed here for Python and the ESP32 host build with `dropped == 0`. The undocumented differences are the findings above.
2. Conformance: Python and the ESP32 host build pass (run here); .NET read only.
3. API surface: all three were first published on 9 Nov (tags `v0.9.0b1` at 385a6e6, `v0.9.0-beta.1` at 23af50b in .NET and at 982c544 in Arduino). The 2 Nov H4 was fixed before that: the package id is `Wbskt` (src/Wbskt/Wbskt.csproj:11), the `Socket*` protocol types are renamed and internal (src/Wbskt/Protocol/*.cs), and only the capability records are public. No repository has a changelog, and master is ahead of every tag with the version unchanged (docs and packaging commits only). What should still change before 1.0 is in the findings.
4. Thread and asyncio safety: findings 1, 3 and 4. Checked and sound: the .NET flush has no lost wake-up (WbsktClient.cs:291-321 re-checks after releasing the lock), the Python drain cannot mark a closed connection connected (cancellation lands at the next await), the ESP32 sign-in task hands over through one atomic flag and `end()` waits for it (WbsktClient.cpp:91-95, 205-232), and OTA checks the SHA-256 before `Update.end` makes the image bootable (WbsktOtaFlash.cpp:149-156). The 2 Nov L7 is fixed: the trial clock counts Wi-Fi time only (WbsktClient.cpp:105-116).
5. Runtimes and boards: see the runtimes finding. The ESP32 README's claim of ESP32, S3 and C3 matches the CI matrix (.github/workflows/ci.yml:66-69), and PlatformIO's `esp32dev` job builds against Arduino core 2.x while the Arduino CLI job builds 3.x, so "2.x or 3.x" (README.md:54) is covered.
6. Versions and releases: the versions agree in every file (Python `version.py` 0.9.0b1 read by hatch, pyproject.toml:39-40; .NET `Directory.Build.props:3` used by Wbskt.csproj:12; Arduino 0.9.0-beta.1 in library.properties, library.json and src/wbskt/Version.h, enforced by scripts/check-version.sh in CI). All three release workflows refuse a tag that differs from the version or is not on master before running the suites. PyPI and NuGet use trusted publishing with no stored key, as their documents say. PlatformIO uses a stored token, which the repository's CONTRIBUTING.md says and Docs/SDK.Releasing.md does not, and that workflow has never run (finding).
7. Samples: the Arduino README samples, the three `examples/` sketches and the site's Arduino, C# and Python snippets all match today's signatures, apart from the placeholders. The ones that do not run are the two Python findings, the get-started sketch, the PlatformIO install line and the .NET README's missing `using`.

### What each lane could not check

- **Auth and management:** nothing .NET was built or run (no SDK in the container), so every finding is from reading code and SQL. Not read: `EventLogCsv.cs`, `ClientReadingsCsv.cs`, `EventProvider.cs` and `ClientProvider.cs` beyond the presence, state and capability calls, the integration providers and secret store, `DeletedWorkspaceSweeper`, the bodies of `Models/` contract records (only the changed file list), `Docs/Auth.Host.E2E.Scenarios.md` beyond a search, and the test bodies. The clone is shallow, so the `[SignalRPrivate]` modifier could not be dated. The console issue dump has titles only, so "filed" for the console finding rests on titles.
- **Credits and usage:** nothing .NET or SQL could be run (no SDK, no SQL Server), so every finding is from reading. Whether the main database runs with `READ_COMMITTED_SNAPSHOT` is not in the repo files read, so how long `Run_Start` waits behind `RunCredits_Post` is not known. Production numbers (#619) do not exist yet.
- **Billing and pricing:** (1) Nothing was built or run: there is no .NET SDK here, so every claim about the C# and SQL is from reading. (2) Dodo's dashboard: the price, currency, billing period and tax-inclusive setting of each product, which webhook events the endpoint is subscribed to, the business-level past-due grace and plan-change failure default, and whether adaptive currency is on. So the prices in the table below are compared between the docs and the site only; none is in the code. (3) What a live Dodo subscription payment looks like (`product_cart`, metadata on renewals); Dodo's public API reference and webhook guide were read for the schema, the retry schedule and customer matching. (4) Production environment values (`Billing__Plans__*`, product ids). (5) The pricing write-up linked from #698 (a claude.ai artifact); only the issue body was read. (6) Capacity: only the two named project files were read; their numbers are a model dated 29 October, not a measurement.
- **Tenants and access:** nothing .NET could be built or run (no SDK in the container), so every claim is from reading the code and SQL; the order `Tenant_GetForUser` returns rows in and the real bus's behaviour with the broker down are inferred. The management host's providers were not read procedure by procedure for workspace scoping, and `CredentialRetentionService`, `AccountProvider` and Docs/API.Endpoints.md were not read. Index and lock findings, where any are implied, come from code alone.
- **Gateways:** nothing .NET could be built or run (no SDK in the container), so every claim is from reading. MQTTnet's behaviour was read from the `master` branch of dotnet/MQTTnet on GitHub, not from the pinned 5.2.0.1603 tag (the tag could not be fetched); findings that depend on it say so. Also not checked: whether queued commands keep their order across the bus hop to the gateway (they are published oldest first, one event each; the consumer's concurrency was not read); how Traefik treats a dead client on a TCP router; whether `Clients/Wbskt.Simulator` and `Tests/Wbskt.LoadTester` compile against the published SDK (package version and `ClientConfig` shape compared by grep only); the SDK repos' links to the old Device.Protocol pages.
- **Client libraries:** nothing .NET was built or run (no .NET SDK in the container), so every wbskt.dotnet claim is from reading. The PlatformIO registry, PyPI and nuget.org were not queried; release state comes from Git tags and GitHub Actions run lists only. On-device ESP32 behaviour was not run; the Arduino client ran only as its Linux host build over shims. The site was read, not built.
- **Workflow engine:** nothing .NET could be built or run in this container (no SDK), so every claim comes from reading the code on `origin/master`; nothing was reproduced. The lane's budget ran out before these were read, so they had no pass at all: `WorkflowLifecycleService` (publish, rollback, deprecate), `WorkflowRunQueryService` and `WorkflowQueryService`, `PublicCallbackController`, `SharedVariableService`, `HistoryRetentionGc`, `TriggerOutcomeBuffer`/`TriggerOutcomeFlusher`, `ClientHoldRecorder`, `ClientPresenceTicker`/`ClientPresenceParker`, `ExpressionEvaluator`, the Fork, Join, ForEach, ParallelForEach, Variable, Logic, End and FailRun executors, the Fail and Fork result handlers, `Tests/Wbskt.Workflow.Engine.Host.IntegrationTests`, the workflow scenarios in `Tests/Wbskt.E2E.FeatureTests`, and `Docs/Workflow.Engine/README.md`. Which version a waiting run resumes on after a publish was not re-checked beyond the open issue #595. How MassTransit behaves on a publish with the broker down and whether it cancels consumers on stop are inferred.
- **Database:** nothing .NET or SQL could be run (no SDK, no SQL Server), so every claim is from reading. The production export from wbskt/Wbskt#619 does not exist yet (the issue is still open), so **index and deadlock findings come from code alone**: no index usage numbers, no missing-index hints and no deadlock graphs were read. Every "no reader" verdict in the index table should be checked against `sys.dm_db_index_usage_stats` before an index is dropped, and every lock-order inversion against the deadlock graphs.
- **Console and site:** nothing was run against a live backend and there is no .NET SDK here, so every contract finding comes from reading both sides (console TypeScript and backend C#/SQL). The console was built and linted in a scratch copy (`npm ci`, `npx ng build`, `npx eslint .`: build passes with 10 style-budget warnings, lint has 0 errors and 14 warnings) and the site was built in a scratch copy (`npm run build`: 62 routes prerendered, no failures). The privacy page's "reply within one business day" promise and the competitor prices in the home comparison table cannot be checked from code. SignalR's default retry schedule is taken from the library's documentation, not re-read from its source. Out-of-order connect and disconnect events were not checked against the gateway.
- **Infra and CI:** GitHub repository settings (environment protection rules and required reviewers, branch protection, default token permissions, which checks are required, whether Dependabot alerts are on); the VM's real `.env`, its running containers, its crontab and whether it answers from outside; Traefik's behaviour by running it; which NuGet package the scan flagged (the log prints project paths only and the advisory API is not reachable from here); `Deploy-Databases.ps1` (not opened); anything that needs a .NET build (no SDK in this container); the values in the project's `dev.env` (names only, by rule). Everything below is from reading code, config and GitHub run data.
